Pensou por um segundo

Perfeito — segurança básica ficou redonda ✅

ping confirmou que a função está com:

SUPABASE_URL ✅

SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY ✅

IMPORT_TOKEN ✅

e ainda consegue gerar signed URL do bucket db ✅

import cards funcionando com allowlist ✅

Agora, pra fechar o “pacote segurança” (sem complicar demais), eu faria estes 4 hardenings rápidos:

1) Rotacionar segredos (se ainda não fez)

Como você já expôs token/keys aqui, trate como comprometido:

1.1 Rotacionar IMPORT_TOKEN
supabase secrets set IMPORT_TOKEN="NOVO_TOKEN_FORTE"
supabase functions deploy import-json
2) Bloquear replay (timestamp + HMAC)

Hoje, se alguém pegar o IMPORT_TOKEN, consegue reexecutar requests.
Solução simples: enviar 2 headers extras:

x-ts: unix timestamp (segundos)

x-sig: HMAC SHA-256 do ts + "\n" + rawBody com uma secret (IMPORT_SIGNING_KEY)

2.1 Setar secret nova
supabase secrets set IMPORT_SIGNING_KEY="UMA_CHAVE_FORTE_ALEATORIA"
supabase functions deploy import-json
2.2 Patch no index.ts (logo após ler raw)

Adicione:

const SIGNING_KEY = Deno.env.get("IMPORT_SIGNING_KEY") ?? "";
if (!SIGNING_KEY) return json(500, { ok:false, step:"env", error:"Missing IMPORT_SIGNING_KEY" });

const ts = req.headers.get("x-ts") ?? "";
const sig = req.headers.get("x-sig") ?? "";
const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
const tsNum = Number(ts);

// janela de 5 min
if (!Number.isFinite(tsNum) || Math.abs(now - tsNum) > 300) {
  return json(401, { ok:false, step:"sig.ts", error:"Invalid or expired timestamp" });
}

// calcula HMAC(ts + "\n" + raw)
const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
  "raw",
  new TextEncoder().encode(SIGNING_KEY),
  { name: "HMAC", hash: "SHA-256" },
  false,
  ["sign"]
);
const mac = await crypto.subtle.sign(
  "HMAC",
  key,
  new TextEncoder().encode(`${ts}\n${raw}`)
);
const expected = btoa(String.fromCharCode(...new Uint8Array(mac)));

if (sig !== expected) {
  return json(401, { ok:false, step:"sig.hmac", error:"Invalid signature" });
}

Isso torna o token inútil sem a assinatura, e evita replay fora da janela.

3) Rate-limit simples

Mesmo com HMAC, vale limitar:

por IP (Cloudflare header) ou

por “chave do token”

Uma forma rápida sem infra extra é uma tabela import_audit + checar “últimos N minutos”. Se você quiser, eu te mando a SQL e o trecho TS.

4) Trancar o Storage

Você já está usando bucket privado + service role, ótimo. Só garanta:

bucket db private

sem policy pública em storage.objects

SQL para listar policies:

select schemaname, tablename, policyname, roles, cmd, qual
from pg_policies
where schemaname='storage' and tablename='objects';
Observação sobre arquivos aqui no chat

Alguns arquivos que você tinha enviado anteriormente expiraram (limitação do chat). Se você quiser que eu valide novamente algum JSON/CSV por aqui, reenvie os arquivos.

Se você disser “quero o anti-replay”, eu já te devolvo o patch pronto aplicado no seu index.ts + o template PowerShell pra calcular x-ts e x-sig na chamada.

aplique o patch do token nesse código

import { createClient } from "https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2";

type Body =
  | { kind: "ping"; bucket?: string; path?: string }
  | { kind: "cards"; bucket: string; path: string; source?: string }
  | { kind: "tournamentMeta"; bucket: string; path: string; source?: string };

const corsHeaders: Record<string, string> = {
  "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",
  "Access-Control-Allow-Headers":
    "authorization, apikey, content-type, x-import-token",
  "Access-Control-Allow-Methods": "POST, OPTIONS",
  "Content-Type": "application/json; charset=utf-8",
};

// ✅ Security allowlist
const ALLOWED_BUCKET = "db";
const ALLOWED_PATHS = new Set(["cards.json", "tournamentMeta.json"]);

function json(status: number, payload: unknown) {
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2), {
    status,
    headers: corsHeaders,
  });
}

function errInfo(err: any) {
  return {
    message: err?.message ?? String(err),
    details: err?.details ?? null,
    hint: err?.hint ?? null,
    code: err?.code ?? null,
    status: err?.status ?? null,
    name: err?.name ?? null,
    stack: err?.stack ?? null,
  };
}

function chunk<T>(arr: T[], size: number): T[][] {
  const out: T[][] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i += size) out.push(arr.slice(i, i + size));
  return out;
}

Deno.serve(async (req) => {
  if (req.method === "OPTIONS")
    return new Response(null, { status: 204, headers: corsHeaders });

  try {
    if (req.method !== "POST")
      return json(405, {
        ok: false,
        step: "method",
        error: "Method not allowed",
      });

    const SUPABASE_URL = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL") ?? "";
    const SERVICE_ROLE = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY") ?? "";
    const IMPORT_TOKEN = Deno.env.get("IMPORT_TOKEN") ?? "";

    if (!SUPABASE_URL)
      return json(500, { ok: false, step: "env", error: "Missing SUPABASE_URL" });
    if (!SERVICE_ROLE)
      return json(500, {
        ok: false,
        step: "env",
        error: "Missing SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY",
      });
    if (!IMPORT_TOKEN)
      return json(500, {
        ok: false,
        step: "env",
        error: "Missing IMPORT_TOKEN",
      });

    const token = req.headers.get("x-import-token") ?? "";
    if (token !== IMPORT_TOKEN)
      return json(401, {
        ok: false,
        step: "auth",
        error: "Invalid x-import-token",
      });

    const raw = await req.text();
    let body: Body;
    try {
      body = JSON.parse(raw);
    } catch (e) {
      return json(400, { ok: false, step: "parse.json", error: errInfo(e), raw });
    }

    // ✅ Security guard (bucket/path allowlist)
    // - for ping: only validate if bucket+path were provided
    // - for non-ping: always validate (bucket/path are required by type)
    if (body.kind === "ping") {
      if (body.bucket && body.path) {
        if (body.bucket !== ALLOWED_BUCKET) {
          return json(403, { ok: false, step: "guard.bucket", error: "bucket not allowed" });
        }
        if (!ALLOWED_PATHS.has(body.path)) {
          return json(403, { ok: false, step: "guard.path", error: "path not allowed" });
        }
      }
    } else {
      // body.kind === "cards" | "tournamentMeta"
      if (body.bucket !== ALLOWED_BUCKET) {
        return json(403, { ok: false, step: "guard.bucket", error: "bucket not allowed" });
      }
      if (!ALLOWED_PATHS.has(body.path)) {
        return json(403, { ok: false, step: "guard.path", error: "path not allowed" });
      }
    }

    const supabase = createClient(SUPABASE_URL, SERVICE_ROLE, {
      auth: { persistSession: false },
    });

    if (body.kind === "ping") {
      const info: any = {
        ok: true,
        step: "ping",
        env: {
          hasSupabaseUrl: !!SUPABASE_URL,
          hasServiceRole: !!SERVICE_ROLE,
          hasImportToken: !!IMPORT_TOKEN,
        },
      };

      if (body.bucket && body.path) {
        const { data, error } = await supabase.storage
          .from(body.bucket)
          .createSignedUrl(body.path, 60);
        if (error)
          return json(500, {
            ok: false,
            step: "storage.createSignedUrl",
            error: errInfo(error),
          });
        info.storageProbe = {
          signedUrlCreated: true,
          expiresIn: 60,
          urlPrefix: data?.signedUrl?.slice(0, 60) ?? null,
        };
      }

      return json(200, info);
    }

    // non-ping: bucket/path guaranteed
    const { data: blob, error: dlErr } = await supabase.storage
      .from(body.bucket)
      .download(body.path);
    if (dlErr)
      return json(500, { ok: false, step: "storage.download", error: errInfo(dlErr) });

    const text = await blob.text();
    let fileJson: any;
    try {
      fileJson = JSON.parse(text);
    } catch (e) {
      return json(500, { ok: false, step: "parse.file_json", error: errInfo(e) });
    }

    if (body.kind === "cards") {
      const cards = Array.isArray(fileJson) ? fileJson : fileJson?.cards;
      if (!Array.isArray(cards))
        return json(400, {
          ok: false,
          step: "cards.shape",
          error: "Expected array (or {cards:[]})",
        });

      let affected = 0;
      for (const part of chunk(cards, 400)) {
        const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("import_cards_json", {
          cards_json: part,
          source_override: body.source ?? "dreamborn",
        });
        if (error)
          return json(500, {
            ok: false,
            step: "rpc.import_cards_json",
            error: errInfo(error),
          });
        affected += Number(data ?? 0);
      }
      return json(200, { ok: true, kind: "cards", affected });
    }

    if (body.kind === "tournamentMeta") {
      const decks = Array.isArray(fileJson?.decks) ? fileJson.decks : [];
      if (!Array.isArray(decks))
        return json(400, {
          ok: false,
          step: "tournamentMeta.shape",
          error: "Expected {decks:[]}",
        });

      let decksAffected = 0;
      for (const part of chunk(decks, 40)) {
        const rows = part
          .filter((d: any) => d?.url)
          .map((d: any) => ({
            url: d.url,
            source_url: d.url,
            archetype: d.archetype ?? null,
            author: d.author ?? null,
            cards: d.cards ?? [],
            total_cards: d.totalQty ?? null,
            source: body.source ?? "inkdecks",
            scraped_at: new Date().toISOString(),
            updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
          }));

        if (rows.length) {
          const { error } = await supabase.from("decks").upsert(rows, { onConflict: "url" });
          if (error)
            return json(500, {
              ok: false,
              step: "db.upsert.decks",
              error: errInfo(error),
            });
          decksAffected += rows.length;
        }
      }
      return json(200, { ok: true, kind: "tournamentMeta", decksAffected });
    }

    return json(400, { ok: false, step: "input", error: "Invalid kind" });
  } catch (e) {
    console.error("IMPORT-JSON ERROR", e);
    return json(500, { ok: false, step: "catch", error: errInfo(e) });
  }
});
Pensou por um segundo

Aí vai o mesmo código, com o patch de anti-replay + assinatura (timestamp + HMAC) aplicado.

✅ O que muda:

Exige 3 headers para qualquer request (inclusive ping):

x-import-token (o seu token atual)

x-ts (timestamp unix em segundos)

x-sig (HMAC SHA-256 em Base64 de "<ts>\n<rawBody>" usando IMPORT_SIGNING_KEY)

Janela de validade: 5 minutos (±300s)

Atualiza CORS para permitir x-ts e x-sig

index.ts com patch aplicado
import { createClient } from "https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2";

type Body =
  | { kind: "ping"; bucket?: string; path?: string }
  | { kind: "cards"; bucket: string; path: string; source?: string }
  | { kind: "tournamentMeta"; bucket: string; path: string; source?: string };

const corsHeaders: Record<string, string> = {
  "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",
  "Access-Control-Allow-Headers":
    "authorization, apikey, content-type, x-import-token, x-ts, x-sig",
  "Access-Control-Allow-Methods": "POST, OPTIONS",
  "Content-Type": "application/json; charset=utf-8",
};

// ✅ Security allowlist
const ALLOWED_BUCKET = "db";
const ALLOWED_PATHS = new Set(["cards.json", "tournamentMeta.json"]);

// ✅ Anti-replay settings
const MAX_SKEW_SECONDS = 300; // 5 minutos

function json(status: number, payload: unknown) {
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2), {
    status,
    headers: corsHeaders,
  });
}

function errInfo(err: any) {
  return {
    message: err?.message ?? String(err),
    details: err?.details ?? null,
    hint: err?.hint ?? null,
    code: err?.code ?? null,
    status: err?.status ?? null,
    name: err?.name ?? null,
    stack: err?.stack ?? null,
  };
}

function chunk<T>(arr: T[], size: number): T[][] {
  const out: T[][] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i += size) out.push(arr.slice(i, i + size));
  return out;
}

function base64FromArrayBuffer(buf: ArrayBuffer): string {
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(buf);
  let bin = "";
  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i++) bin += String.fromCharCode(bytes[i]);
  return btoa(bin);
}

function timingSafeEqual(a: string, b: string): boolean {
  if (a.length !== b.length) return false;
  let out = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) out |= a.charCodeAt(i) ^ b.charCodeAt(i);
  return out === 0;
}

async function verifySignedRequest(
  req: Request,
  rawBody: string,
  signingKey: string,
) {
  const ts = req.headers.get("x-ts") ?? "";
  const sig = req.headers.get("x-sig") ?? "";

  if (!ts || !sig) {
    return { ok: false, step: "sig.missing", error: "Missing x-ts or x-sig" };
  }

  const tsNum = Number(ts);
  if (!Number.isFinite(tsNum)) {
    return { ok: false, step: "sig.ts", error: "Invalid x-ts" };
  }

  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  if (Math.abs(now - tsNum) > MAX_SKEW_SECONDS) {
    return { ok: false, step: "sig.ts", error: "Expired timestamp (±5min)" };
  }

  const enc = new TextEncoder();
  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
    "raw",
    enc.encode(signingKey),
    { name: "HMAC", hash: "SHA-256" },
    false,
    ["sign"],
  );

  const msg = `${ts}\n${rawBody}`;
  const mac = await crypto.subtle.sign("HMAC", key, enc.encode(msg));
  const expected = base64FromArrayBuffer(mac);

  if (!timingSafeEqual(sig, expected)) {
    return { ok: false, step: "sig.hmac", error: "Invalid signature" };
  }

  return { ok: true };
}

Deno.serve(async (req) => {
  if (req.method === "OPTIONS")
    return new Response(null, { status: 204, headers: corsHeaders });

  try {
    if (req.method !== "POST")
      return json(405, {
        ok: false,
        step: "method",
        error: "Method not allowed",
      });

    const SUPABASE_URL = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL") ?? "";
    const SERVICE_ROLE = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY") ?? "";
    const IMPORT_TOKEN = Deno.env.get("IMPORT_TOKEN") ?? "";
    const SIGNING_KEY = Deno.env.get("IMPORT_SIGNING_KEY") ?? "";

    if (!SUPABASE_URL)
      return json(500, { ok: false, step: "env", error: "Missing SUPABASE_URL" });
    if (!SERVICE_ROLE)
      return json(500, {
        ok: false,
        step: "env",
        error: "Missing SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY",
      });
    if (!IMPORT_TOKEN)
      return json(500, { ok: false, step: "env", error: "Missing IMPORT_TOKEN" });
    if (!SIGNING_KEY)
      return json(500, { ok: false, step: "env", error: "Missing IMPORT_SIGNING_KEY" });

    // Gate extra (token)
    const token = req.headers.get("x-import-token") ?? "";
    if (token !== IMPORT_TOKEN)
      return json(401, { ok: false, step: "auth", error: "Invalid x-import-token" });

    // Lê body RAW (necessário pra assinatura)
    const raw = await req.text();

    // ✅ Verifica assinatura anti-replay (ts + hmac)
    const sigCheck = await verifySignedRequest(req, raw, SIGNING_KEY);
    if (!sigCheck.ok) return json(401, { ok: false, step: sigCheck.step, error: sigCheck.error });

    // Parse JSON
    let body: Body;
    try {
      body = JSON.parse(raw);
    } catch (e) {
      return json(400, { ok: false, step: "parse.json", error: errInfo(e), raw });
    }

    // ✅ bucket/path allowlist
    if (body.kind === "ping") {
      if (body.bucket && body.path) {
        if (body.bucket !== ALLOWED_BUCKET)
          return json(403, { ok: false, step: "guard.bucket", error: "bucket not allowed" });
        if (!ALLOWED_PATHS.has(body.path))
          return json(403, { ok: false, step: "guard.path", error: "path not allowed" });
      }
    } else {
      if (body.bucket !== ALLOWED_BUCKET)
        return json(403, { ok: false, step: "guard.bucket", error: "bucket not allowed" });
      if (!ALLOWED_PATHS.has(body.path))
        return json(403, { ok: false, step: "guard.path", error: "path not allowed" });
    }

    const supabase = createClient(SUPABASE_URL, SERVICE_ROLE, {
      auth: { persistSession: false },
    });

    // PING
    if (body.kind === "ping") {
      const info: any = {
        ok: true,
        step: "ping",
        env: {
          hasSupabaseUrl: !!SUPABASE_URL,
          hasServiceRole: !!SERVICE_ROLE,
          hasImportToken: !!IMPORT_TOKEN,
          hasSigningKey: !!SIGNING_KEY,
        },
      };

      if (body.bucket && body.path) {
        const { data, error } = await supabase.storage
          .from(body.bucket)
          .createSignedUrl(body.path, 60);
        if (error)
          return json(500, { ok: false, step: "storage.createSignedUrl", error: errInfo(error) });

        info.storageProbe = {
          signedUrlCreated: true,
          expiresIn: 60,
          urlPrefix: data?.signedUrl?.slice(0, 60) ?? null,
        };
      }
      return json(200, info);
    }

    // DOWNLOAD
    const { data: blob, error: dlErr } = await supabase.storage
      .from(body.bucket)
      .download(body.path);
    if (dlErr) return json(500, { ok: false, step: "storage.download", error: errInfo(dlErr) });

    const text = await blob.text();
    let fileJson: any;
    try {
      fileJson = JSON.parse(text);
    } catch (e) {
      return json(500, { ok: false, step: "parse.file_json", error: errInfo(e) });
    }

    // IMPORT CARDS
    if (body.kind === "cards") {
      const cards = Array.isArray(fileJson) ? fileJson : fileJson?.cards;
      if (!Array.isArray(cards))
        return json(400, { ok: false, step: "cards.shape", error: "Expected array (or {cards:[]})" });

      let affected = 0;
      for (const part of chunk(cards, 400)) {
        const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("import_cards_json", {
          cards_json: part,
          source_override: body.source ?? "dreamborn",
        });
        if (error) return json(500, { ok: false, step: "rpc.import_cards_json", error: errInfo(error) });
        affected += Number(data ?? 0);
      }
      return json(200, { ok: true, kind: "cards", affected });
    }

    // IMPORT TOURNAMENT META
    if (body.kind === "tournamentMeta") {
      const decks = Array.isArray(fileJson?.decks) ? fileJson.decks : [];
      if (!Array.isArray(decks))
        return json(400, { ok: false, step: "tournamentMeta.shape", error: "Expected {decks:[]}" });

      let decksAffected = 0;
      for (const part of chunk(decks, 40)) {
        const rows = part
          .filter((d: any) => d?.url)
          .map((d: any) => ({
            url: d.url,
            source_url: d.url,
            archetype: d.archetype ?? null,
            author: d.author ?? null,
            cards: d.cards ?? [],
            total_cards: d.totalQty ?? null,
            source: body.source ?? "inkdecks",
            scraped_at: new Date().toISOString(),
            updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
          }));

        if (rows.length) {
          const { error } = await supabase.from("decks").upsert(rows, { onConflict: "url" });
          if (error) return json(500, { ok: false, step: "db.upsert.decks", error: errInfo(error) });
          decksAffected += rows.length;
        }
      }
      return json(200, { ok: true, kind: "tournamentMeta", decksAffected });
    }

    return json(400, { ok: false, step: "input", error: "Invalid kind" });
  } catch (e) {
    console.error("IMPORT-JSON ERROR", e);
    return json(500, { ok: false, step: "catch", error: errInfo(e) });
  }
});
1) Configure o secret novo
supabase secrets set IMPORT_SIGNING_KEY="SUA_CHAVE_FORTE_ALEATORIA"
supabase functions deploy import-json
2) Como chamar via PowerShell (gera x-ts e x-sig)

Exemplo (funciona tanto pra ping quanto pra cards):

$signingKey = "SUA_CHAVE_FORTE_ALEATORIA"
$importToken = "SEU_IMPORT_TOKEN"

$body = @{ kind="ping"; bucket="db"; path="cards.json" } | ConvertTo-Json -Compress
$ts = [DateTimeOffset]::UtcNow.ToUnixTimeSeconds()

$keyBytes = [Text.Encoding]::UTF8.GetBytes($signingKey)
$hmac = New-Object System.Security.Cryptography.HMACSHA256($keyBytes)

$msg = "$ts`n$body"
$msgBytes = [Text.Encoding]::UTF8.GetBytes($msg)
$sig = [Convert]::ToBase64String($hmac.ComputeHash($msgBytes))

$headers = @{
  "x-import-token" = $importToken
  "x-ts" = "$ts"
  "x-sig" = $sig
  "apikey" = "SUA_SB_PUBLISHABLE"
  "Authorization" = "Bearer SUA_SB_PUBLISHABLE"
}

Invoke-RestMethod `
  -Uri "https://bjfxtfchfawvkaufdtdn.supabase.co/functions/v1/import-json" `
  -Method Post `
  -Headers $headers `
  -ContentType "application/json" `
  -Body $body

Se quiser, eu adiciono mais um hardening rápido: allowlist por kind → path (ex.: cards só pode cards.json, tournamentMeta só tournamentMeta.json), pra evitar alguém mandar kind=cards apontando pra outro arquivo “permitido” no futuro.

supabase secrets set IMPORT\_SIGNING\_KEY\=

Queries prontas
Posso te passar 3 queries “final”:

Buscar decks que contenham um conjunto de card_ids (ou nomes)

Últimos torneios por formato (com player_count, date, link)

Últimos torneios por formato + campeão (deck placement = 1)
Pensou por um segundo

Aí vão as 3 queries finais, prontas pra colar no SQL Editor do Supabase (sem $1, sem parâmetros), com templates fáceis de preencher.

Obs.: usei player_count (é o nome correto no seu schema) e evitei players.

1) Buscar decks que contenham um conjunto de card_id (ALL)

Substitua os VALUES (...) pelos card_id que você quer (ex.: "1/C1 • EN • 1").

WITH input(card_id) AS (
  VALUES
    ('1/C1 • EN • 1'),
    ('2/C3 • EN • 1')
),
wanted AS (
  SELECT COUNT(*)::int AS n FROM input
),
matched_decks AS (
  SELECT dc.deck_id
  FROM deck_cards dc
  JOIN input i ON i.card_id = dc.card_id
  GROUP BY dc.deck_id
  HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT i.card_id) = (SELECT n FROM wanted)   -- <-- contém TODAS
)
SELECT
  d.id,
  d.name       AS deck_name,
  d.author,
  d.archetype,
  d.inks,
  d.placement,
  d.url        AS deck_url,
  t.id         AS tournament_id,
  t.name       AS tournament_name,
  t.format,
  t.date,
  t.player_count,
  t.url        AS tournament_url
FROM matched_decks md
JOIN decks d ON d.id = md.deck_id
LEFT JOIN tournaments t ON t.id = d.tournament_id
ORDER BY t.date DESC NULLS LAST, d.placement NULLS LAST, d.scraped_at DESC NULLS LAST
LIMIT 200;
Variante “ANY” (retorna decks que tenham qualquer uma das cartas)

Troque o matched_decks por:

matched_decks AS (
  SELECT DISTINCT dc.deck_id
  FROM deck_cards dc
  JOIN input i ON i.card_id = dc.card_id
)
1b) Buscar decks por nome de carta (ALL)

Útil quando card_id estiver faltando em alguns registros.

WITH input(card_name) AS (
  VALUES
    ('Dragon Fire'),
    ('Tinker Bell - Giant Fairy')
),
wanted AS (
  SELECT COUNT(*)::int AS n FROM input
),
matched_decks AS (
  SELECT dc.deck_id
  FROM deck_cards dc
  JOIN input i ON lower(i.card_name) = lower(dc.card_name)
  GROUP BY dc.deck_id
  HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT lower(i.card_name)) = (SELECT n FROM wanted)
)
SELECT
  d.id,
  d.name AS deck_name,
  d.author,
  d.archetype,
  d.inks,
  d.placement,
  d.url AS deck_url
FROM matched_decks md
JOIN decks d ON d.id = md.deck_id
ORDER BY d.scraped_at DESC NULLS LAST
LIMIT 200;
2) Últimos torneios por formato (com player_count, date, link)

Ajuste rn <= 10 para quantos por formato você quiser.

SELECT
  format,
  id,
  name,
  date,
  player_count,
  url
FROM (
  SELECT
    t.*,
    ROW_NUMBER() OVER (
      PARTITION BY t.format
      ORDER BY t.date DESC NULLS LAST, t.scraped_at DESC NULLS LAST
    ) AS rn
  FROM tournaments t
) x
WHERE x.rn <= 10
ORDER BY x.format, x.date DESC NULLS LAST;
3) Últimos torneios por formato + campeão (placement = 1)

Campeão = deck com placement = 1.
Incluí um join “tolerante” via tournament_id ou tournament_url (caso você tenha decks sem tournament_id preenchido).

WITH last_t AS (
  SELECT
    t.*,
    ROW_NUMBER() OVER (
      PARTITION BY t.format
      ORDER BY t.date DESC NULLS LAST, t.scraped_at DESC NULLS LAST
    ) AS rn
  FROM tournaments t
),
champ AS (
  SELECT DISTINCT ON (COALESCE(d.tournament_id, d.tournament_url))
    COALESCE(d.tournament_id, d.tournament_url) AS match_key,
    d.id        AS deck_id,
    d.name      AS deck_name,
    d.author,
    d.url       AS deck_url,
    d.archetype,
    d.inks,
    d.record_text,
    d.wins, d.losses, d.draws,
    d.scraped_at
  FROM decks d
  WHERE d.placement = 1
  ORDER BY COALESCE(d.tournament_id, d.tournament_url), d.scraped_at DESC NULLS LAST
)
SELECT
  lt.format,
  lt.id           AS tournament_id,
  lt.name         AS tournament_name,
  lt.date,
  lt.player_count,
  lt.url          AS tournament_url,
  c.deck_id       AS champion_deck_id,
  c.deck_name     AS champion_deck_name,
  c.author        AS champion_author,
  c.deck_url      AS champion_deck_url,
  c.archetype     AS champion_archetype,
  c.inks          AS champion_inks,
  c.record_text,
  c.wins, c.losses, c.draws
FROM last_t lt
LEFT JOIN champ c
  ON c.match_key = lt.id
  OR c.match_key = lt.url
  OR c.match_key = lt.source_url
WHERE lt.rn <= 10
ORDER BY lt.format, lt.date DESC NULLS LAST;

Se você quiser, eu adapto a Query 1 para retornar também:

quantidade total das cartas buscadas no deck,

lista das cartas faltantes (quando não bate ALL),

e/ou filtros por format, min_player_count, date >= ....

query 1 não retornou valores

Success. No rows returned
Pensou por um segundo

Isso acontece por 2 motivos bem comuns:

Você passou card_id que não existe em deck_cards.card_id (às vezes por diferença de espaços/“•”/NBSP), ou

A query 1 está no modo “ALL” (tem que conter todas as cartas). Se uma delas não aparece em nenhum deck, o resultado vira vazio.

Vamos diagnosticar em 30s com 3 queries rápidas (cole no SQL Editor).

A) Confirme que deck_cards tem dados
select
  count(*) as deck_cards_total,
  count(distinct deck_id) as decks_with_lines,
  count(distinct card_id) filter (where card_id is not null) as distinct_card_ids,
  count(*) filter (where card_id is null) as rows_card_id_null
from public.deck_cards;
B) Ache o card_id correto pelo nome (pega o ID “canônico” da tabela cards)

Ex.: “Dragon Fire”:

select id, name
from public.cards
where name ilike '%Dragon Fire%'
order by name
limit 50;

Copie o id retornado e use ele na query 1.

C) Verifique se o card_id existe em deck_cards

Substitua pelos IDs que você está usando:

select card_id, count(*) as cnt
from public.deck_cards
where card_id in (
  '1/C1 • EN • 1',
  '2/C3 • EN • 1'
)
group by card_id
order by cnt desc;

Se vier vazio aqui, é 100% o motivo de “no rows”.

D) Se for problema de espaço/“•”, use match normalizado (sem espaços)

Substitua os IDs e compare assim:

with input(card_id) as (
  values
    ('1/C1 • EN • 1'),
    ('2/C3 • EN • 1')
),
input_norm as (
  select regexp_replace(card_id, '\s+', '', 'g') as nid
  from input
)
select dc.card_id, count(*) as cnt
from public.deck_cards dc
join input_norm i
  on regexp_replace(dc.card_id, '\s+', '', 'g') = i.nid
group by dc.card_id
order by cnt desc;
Query 1 (versão “ANY” primeiro, pra validar)

Se você quer só garantir que encontra algo, teste ANY:

with input(card_id) as (
  values
    ('COLE_O_ID_1'),
    ('COLE_O_ID_2')
)
select
  d.id,
  d.name as deck_name,
  d.author,
  d.archetype,
  d.inks,
  d.placement,
  d.url as deck_url
from public.decks d
where exists (
  select 1
  from public.deck_cards dc
  join input i on i.card_id = dc.card_id
  where dc.deck_id = d.id
)
order by d.scraped_at desc nulls last
limit 200;

Se ANY retornar, aí você volta para ALL (a versão que eu te passei antes) quando quiser “decks que contenham todas as cartas”.

Alguns arquivos que você tinha enviado aqui no chat expiraram; se você quiser que eu valide novamente algum JSON/CSV por aqui, vai precisar reenviar.
